0317 — dumbbell incline curl v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0317 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

An incline dumbbell curl on a bench. Your arms hang behind the body to stretch the biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on an incline bench with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing forward and arms fully extended.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0317.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Sit on an incline bench with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing forward and arms fully extended.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0317.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

For an otherwise unspecified seated setup, place the feet in a stable comfortable support position; preserve any source-defined footrests, pedals, leg pads or floor contact.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 0317.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair, Adjustable utility bench, Incline bench configuration. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0317.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on an incline bench with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing forward and arms fully extended. 0317.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keeping your upper arms stationary, exhale and curl the weights while contracting your biceps. 0317.json#/instructions/1
  3. Continue to raise the dumbbells until your biceps are fully contracted and the dumbbells are at shoulder level. 0317.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold the contracted position for a brief pause as you squeeze your biceps. 0317.json#/instructions/3
  5. Inhale and slowly begin to lower the dumbbells back to the starting position. 0317.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0317.json#/instructions/5

0318 — dumbbell incline curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0318 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

The incline dumbbell curl. You sit back on an inclined bench and curl up, stretching the biceps at the start.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set an incline bench to a 45-degree angle and sit on it with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing forward. Rest your upper arms on the incline bench and let your elbows hang down, fully extending your arms.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0318.json#/instructions/0, 0318.json#/instructions/1

0319 — dumbbell incline fly

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0319 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A dumbbell fly on an incline bench. The arms open in an arc to work the upper chest.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set an incline bench to a 45-degree angle. Sit on the bench with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing each other. Lie back on the bench and press the dumbbells up to the starting position, directly above your chest.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0319.json#/instructions/0, 0319.json#/instructions/1, 0319.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Sit on the bench with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing each other.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0319.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

For an otherwise unspecified seated setup, place the feet in a stable comfortable support position; preserve any source-defined footrests, pedals, leg pads or floor contact.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 0319.json#/instructions/1

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair, Adjustable utility bench, Incline bench configuration. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0319.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set an incline bench to a 45-degree angle. 0319.json#/instructions/0
  2. Sit on the bench with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing each other. 0319.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lie back on the bench and press the dumbbells up to the starting position, directly above your chest. 0319.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower the dumbbells out to the sides in a wide arc until you feel a stretch in your chest. 0319.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment, then squeeze your chest muscles to bring the dumbbells back up to the starting position. 0319.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0319.json#/instructions/5

0320 — dumbbell incline hammer curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0320 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A hammer curl performed sitting back on an incline bench. The neutral grip works the brachialis and biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on an incline bench with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing your torso and arms fully extended.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0320.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

  3. While keeping your upper arms stationary, exhale and curl the weights while contracting your biceps. 0320.json#/instructions/2

0321 — dumbbell incline hammer press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0321 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

An incline press with dumbbells held palms facing each other. The neutral grip focuses on the upper chest and spares the shoulders.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set an incline bench to a 45-degree angle and sit on it with a dumbbell in each hand, resting on your thighs. Lie back on the bench and position the dumbbells at shoulder level with your palms facing each other.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0321.json#/instructions/0, 0321.json#/instructions/1

  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch legs and repeat. 0410.json#/instructions/4

  5. Inhale and slowly begin to lower the dumbbells back to the starting position. 0416.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0416.json#/instructions/5
